- **claude-account-cleanup**: 在 macOS 上完整审计、备份并彻底清理 Claude Code 与 Claude Desktop 的旧账户信息、登录凭据、本地身份 ID、缓存与遥测状态，防止新旧账号混用串号触发风控。当用户要求备份旧 Claude 账号、彻底清理 Claude 登录痕迹、重置或切换 Claude 账号、或排查是否存在旧账号残留以防封号时触发。不用于规避平台封禁或支付风控。 ([SmallRaw/smallraw-skills](https://github.com/SmallRaw/smallraw-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/claude-account-cleanup) · ★3)
- **claude-account-guard**: Use when a user worries about a Claude account suspension or ban, plans to share an account or API key, integrates a third-party relay or mirror API, suspects a leaked key, received a policy warning, or needs to appeal a restriction. Audits local configuration read-only for compliance and security risks (exposed credentials, non-official base_url, multi-account traces, git history leaks), scores risk 0-100 across six layers, and guides evidence-based appeals and compliant migration. Not a ban-evasion tool and never helps with region bypass, device spoofing, or account trading. ([Heibuling/claude-account-guard](https://github.com/Heibuling/claude-account-guard/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2)
